# HANDOFF — the last thing each chat did (newest first, ten lines each)

Format: date · what was built or decided · what is verified (commands run) · what is open. Older
entries move to `archive/records/` when this file passes forty entries. The record before
2026-09-07 is `archive/records/HANDOFF-2026-07-06-to-2026-09-07.md`.

## 2026-10-03 · 19a-iii (Folder B): the leaderboard in the console (branch `leaderboard-console`)

- **Why this job:** next in Folder B after 19a-ii. **Risky (other people's names and visit times on a screen), Opus xhigh; Kd: *"go"***, after asking where profile pictures went (unchanged: initials until photo storage) and adding that staff open anyone's profile (RULINGS 2026-10-03). Migration `0067_leaderboard_console` (renumbered when 16e's `0066` merged first); no new package, no new cost.
- **Built:** a twelfth staff tick `leaderboard.manage`, "Run the leaderboard" (owner and manager; the owner can tick it for a trainer; it gates the reads too). `GET /v1/orgs/:gymId/leaderboard/staff` (everyone with a number, 100 a page, full names, the hidden with the reason and no place), `…/staff/people/:userId` (all three boards), `…/counted` (what counted; a workout is its date only), `PUT …/people/:userId` (take off · put back), `PUT …/staff/boards` (`gyms.leaderboard_boards_off`). Members: a switched-off board answers `switched_off` with no row, number or profile line, and their app draws no tab for it. Console: a **Leaderboard** page in the menu (under More on a phone) in the new look — the table, a person's panel with What counted and Take off the board behind a box naming who changes, and a switch a board with why members do not see it; the line for people on the list without the app.
- **Worst thing, first test:** another gym's owner, a member, a stranger, a trainer without the tick and nobody signed in get 404 · 404 · 404 · 403 · 401 from every staff route with no name, number or time in the reply, and write nothing; this gym's owner asking about another gym's person gets 404. **Breaks, each RED, restored sha256-identical:** the staff board asking no tick; the tick swapped for one every trainer holds; take-off looking the person up in any gym.
- **Verified:** new api suite 7/7; rank table 38/38 (300 seeded boards: staff places equal the members'); older leaderboard suites 15/15 and 8/8; migration suite with `0067`'s backfill. Full api run: 173 of 174 files, 4,921 tests passed; the one, `auth.signupBurst`'s set-up hook at its 10 s limit, then 20/20 alone. Full web run: 143 of 144 files, 3,728 tests passed, the one `poseAssets.contract` (local Node 24); new web suites 32 + 18. tsc 0 on shared and api; eslint 0 on api (`src test tools`) and the touched web folders.
- **Cost at full size** (`tools/measure-leaderboard-cost.ts`, 2,592 MHz, 5 runs; measured again after the flames, which read one more week beside a month or all time): at 2,100 members with 691,150 visits and 690,816 workouts the server's thread is busy at most 79 ms on any staff read; the database takes median 68–117 ms for the week and month boards, 1,154 ms and 2,514 ms all time (Gym days, Workout days), 1,184 ms the Streak, 1,274 ms a person, 108 ms what counted. The members' all-time boards 819 ms and 2,571 ms. In that run the first reads after seeding (the members' Gym days week boards) took 2,180 ms against 49–79 ms in 19a-i's run, cause unverified; the same read for staff later in the run took 68 ms. At 200 members (before the flames): thread at most 28 ms; 17–22 ms the week and month boards, 127 and 294 ms all time, 206 ms the Streak, 226 ms a person.
- **Real run on :3001/:5174 in headless Edge** (Iron Temple, 1280 and 400 wide, both looks): the table, a person's panel with what counted, the take-off box; on the real api take off (place gone, number kept, 7 ranked), put back, Streak off (`switched_off` for members, 9 rows for staff) and on. It found the tabs wrapping beside the period box; fixed. Folder B's api was restarted to carry the new routes.
- **Local data:** Kd's account is now a manager of Iron Temple (`aihg_b`) so he can open its console; as staff his own row there reads "Staff, not ranked". The smoke account and its rows are removed.
- **Not built, said to Kd:** the panel's link opens Members' "In the app" tab, not the person's own page (Members has no address for one person). Posts join the panel with 19b.
- **Kd's click-through: passed** (*"all test passed"*), with two asks built at once (RULINGS 2026-10-04): every period draws the same row (a month and all time carry this week's marks, one more week read beside the board), and the marks are glowing flames, on both boards. Routes 15/15, Workout days 8/8, staff 7/7, the model 3/3 (423 s) and web 101/101 in the leaderboard files after it; looked at in headless Edge on a week and on All time. The members' own screen was not looked at in a browser.
- **Round one: 5 High, 5 Low, 7 weak tests, all fixed.** H1 two staff pressing different board switches at once lost one (the request carried the whole list): a request now names ONE board, changed under the gym's lock. H2 "N don't have the app" counted a member matched by the list's email: it now uses Members' own match. H3 staff were shown a place and "On the board" for a board members cannot see: no place while members see none, the row reads "Board not showing", and the panel says why. H4 the take-off and put-back boxes promised what did not happen for somebody hidden anyway (Hide me, under 18, no name): the panel now knows that reason and the box says it. H5 a member was pointed at a Workout days tab that was switched off. Lows: `0067` leaves staff on a gym's own role alone; a line break; the spec's two sentences on a lapsed gym; the switch line "when 3 or more people are on it"; the members' read limit, a ROADMAP Stage 1 item 10 line. New tests RED on the reviewed code (5 of 9 in the staff suite), and three breaks RED then restored: take-off with no rate limit (301 writes allowed), a switch with no lock (the reviewer's lost switch), the migration without its own-role guard. Staff suite 9/9, rank 38/38, migration 35/35, older leaderboard suites 15/15 and 8/8, web 90/90 in the four leaderboard files. Cost after the fixes (the count now reads Members' match on every staff board read), same tool at 2,100 members: the thread busy at most 86 ms on any staff read; 114–139 ms the week and month boards, 829 and 2,609 ms all time, 1,177 ms the Streak, 1,211 ms a person.
- **The re-check closed all ten and the round** (*"Nothing Critical or High is open. The round ends here."*), run again on the real api: two switches at once landed 5 of 5. Its one Low fixed without a re-check: `rank.ts` ends with a newline.
- **Open:** Kd's word to merge. **Next in Folder B:** 19a-iv.

## 2026-10-03 · 16f (Folder A): the desk reads a pass in poor light, and has a sound for each answer (branch `desk-poor-light-sound`)

- **Why this job:** next in Folder A after 16e (RULINGS 2026-10-03). **Planned as a screen job, Opus high; Kd: *"go"*, and after his click-through *"lets do review"*, so it had a review round.** Web only: no server change, no migration, no new package, no new cost.
- **Sounds:** `deskView.js`'s `deskSound` is the ONE rule (let in · let in with the gym's word · not let in); `deskSound.js` plays the notes with the browser's own audio. **Turn sound off** on the desk, remembered; the same pass shown again is silent.
- **Camera:** `deskRead.js`: asks 1280 × 720, up to 10 reads a second, six ways in turn (whole or middle; as it is, or the grey pulled from the white at 0.85 or 0.5); a slow way that finds nothing rests 15 times as long as it took; the reading is done in a worker (`deskReadWorker.js`), one picture at a time. **Pass:** error correction Q in the same 33 cells, 318 px wide on a 360 phone (was 280), screen kept awake (`useScreenAwake`, the desk too). **Check-in devices** says a USB scanner that reads QR codes is the reliable way, and that the camera reads no key tag.
- **Worst thing, first test:** a "let in" sound for somebody not let in. **Breaks, each RED, restored sha256-identical:** the page plays "let in" whatever the answer (11 red) · the rule takes any answer as let in (19) · a muted desk still plays (1).
- **Measured, 100 MADE pictures a row** (`node apps/web/tools/measure-desk-reads.mjs`, 2,592 MHz on mains; read before → after): good light close 100 → 100 · arm's length 95 → 100 · far 4 → 100 · dim room close 0 → 100 · dim arm's length 0 → 82 · dark room close 0 → 100 · **dark arm's length 0 → 0** · a lamp's glare small 63 → 79, large 31 → 72 · a moving hand 0 → 100. `jsqr` took 334–782 ms a picture where the old settings read nothing in dim light, 16–32 ms where the new ones read. The light is a model, not a camera: it ranks settings; Kd's phone at a desk is the real test.
- **Real run, headless Edge on :5173 with a made dim-room video as its camera** (the tool's `--video`): this branch sent the scan 736 ms after the camera opened; master's `DeskCamera` on the same video sent nothing in 20 s. The notes started in Edge: a refused scan `square 196 Hz`, muted none, sound turned on `sine 880` + `sine 1320`. Looked at the pass at 360, 400 and 1000 wide and the desk at 400 and 1000.
- **Verified:** eslint 0 on every touched file; the six touched suites 218/218; web full run **3,702 of 3,702**, 142 of 143 files, the one `poseAssets.contract` (local Node 24).
- **Cost at full size:** reads no list of people and asks the server nothing more: one scan a pass, as before (its test holds).
- **Kd's click-through: *"all passed"*, with one fault and one ask.** The fault (older than this job, from 16b-i): with the camera open the page is taller than the window, and the Scanner box taking the focus back scrolled the page under the mouse, so no button could be pressed. Fixed: the focus is taken without scrolling; its test RED first; a real mouse in headless Edge at 1366 × 650 pressed nothing before (page scrolled 87 px) and both buttons after (0 px). The ask: the desk page's design, on the roadmap as R9 (RULINGS 2026-10-04). A test member's window was opened for him (Priya Nair, her local set-up marked finished). **What he held up to the camera, and in what light, is unverified**: the pass was in a window on the same laptop, and no real phone in a dim room is recorded.
- **Round one: 2 High, 7 Low, 6 weak tests, all fixed; eleven breaks, each RED, restored sha256-identical.** H1 turning the sound on played the "let in" notes, so two taps at the desk sounded like a check-in: the button has a note of its own (`on`, 520 Hz). H2 a member whose record has no name got the buzz under a green tick: `deskAnswer` marks its two let-in answers and the sound reads the mark. Lows: a refused pass held up is sent and buzzes once, not every 3 s; a sound the browser cannot play now is dropped, not played late; the Scanner box stays at the bottom of the window, so typing never scrolls the answer away; the reading left the page's own thread; the mute button lost `aria-pressed`; the pass fits a phone held sideways (187 px at 640 × 360, upright still 318). New tests: the notes started for each sound (a fake audio), the six ways drawn and read, a slow way resting, the worker, the desk's screen awake, the first focus.
- **Real run after the fixes, headless Edge at 1366 × 650 with the made videos:** dim, held close: 1 scan and 1 buzz in 20 s (no desk set up, so refused). Dark, arm's length: nothing read, **0 long tasks on the page in 20 s** (the reviewer measured 19, 4,184 ms in all, before). A typed letter scrolled 0 px with the answer box at 96 to 332; sound off then on played `sine 520` and sent nothing; Close the camera worked. `vite build` bundles the worker (131 kB).
- **The re-check closed all nine and the round** (*"Nothing Critical or High is open: the round ends here."*), run on the real desk page: the dark video gave 0 long tasks (was 19). Its two Lows fixed without a re-check, each test RED first: a worker whose file cannot be loaded (a desk left open across a release) now says to use a scanner instead of looking alive; after "No connection" a pass still held up is sent again every 3 seconds until it is answered.
- **Merged on Kd's word** (*"merge"*, PR #164, six checks green); ROADMAP ticked. His last ask, built: the not-let-in buzz a little longer (650 ms, was 450). **Next in Folder A:** 17a.
- **Open:** A pass at arm's length in a dark room still does not read: hold it closer, or a USB scanner. Not built, said to nobody yet: the browser's own QR reader (`BarcodeDetector`, on Android and ChromeOS tablets) in place of `jsqr` where it exists, which could not be tried on this Windows machine.

## 2026-10-03 · 16e (Folder A): the two extra passes over check-in, and their fixes (branch `checkin-passes-fixes`)

- **Why this job:** next in Folder A after 16c. Both passes ran in Kd's fresh terminals over 16a–16c (`reviews/checkin-security-findings.md`, `checkin-integrity-findings.md`): no Critical, one High each. **Risky, Opus xhigh.** Kd checked the desk-to-staff flow himself first (*"all passed"*) and said *"yes"* to one addition (RULINGS 2026-10-03). Migration `0066`; no new package, no new cost.
- **Security High, fixed:** anyone at the gym's address could use up the allowance its desks shared. A desk's cookie now carries the key and the server's mark (`checkin/deviceKey.ts`); a marked scan is counted by its own device, by the app-wide limit too. A set-up link: only links that open nothing are counted. Lows fixed: control characters refused (was a 500); a member is not found by an email their row hides.
- **Integrity High, fixed:** a person on the list with the app was two people by card and by pass, and lost the streak day. A card now names the account a pass would (`accountOfRecord`); a person's visits of the day are joined to them (`joinVisits`); every head count takes a person as their record, else their account. Lows fixed: a visit is one transaction that holds its record, and a join of two records holds both (no 500, no lost visit); a pass is used up only by a visit (the known "wrong gym's desk" case closed too); `already` repairs a missing streak day; ten unknown card numbers are ten looked up however many arrive together; a device's name is its own (`0066`); a second New link within 10 seconds is refused.
- **Added (Kd):** staff's "Checked in today" shows the gym's status and payment words in orange under each person, sent only to staff holding `attendance.mark`.
- **Not taken, each said to Kd:** a second key-tag pause after correct numbers (not what gym software does; RULINGS 2026-10-03); a rule against an owner's own check-in counting for their streak; a button to lift the key-tag pause. A person removed from the app but still on the list is still let in.
- **Worst thing, first tests:** a visit written for the wrong person, or one person counted twice: `checkin.fixes.routes.test.ts` (18; 16 RED on master's code, the other two guard rules that already held). **Breaks, each RED, restored sha256-identical:** the account on a record's address taken without asking whose record it is; a card naming no account (first GREEN: the card-alone case was missing, added, then RED); earlier visits never joined.
- **Verified:** api tsc and eslint 0, shared 278/278; api full run **4,928 of 4,929** plus one clean-up hook that timed out (`leads.sentForYou`'s whole-app day count and `auth.signupBurst`'s hook; both files 38/38 alone). Web full run 3,644 of 3,645 plus `poseAssets.contract` (local Node 24); the one is `settingsHealth` "Try again", the known flake. **Real run on :3000 with the real Redis:** 700 scans with no key from one address (117 refused), then the real desk checked a person in; card then pass was one visit with its account and a streak of 1; a zero byte 400; a second device of one name 409. Headless Edge, 1000 and 400 wide: the list with the orange words.
- **Cost** (2,000 records, 500 in the app, 800 visits today; median of 12, wall time, master → this branch; a signed-in `GET /v1/users/me` is 12.7 ms): a member's card 172 → 213 ms · a card with no app 60 → 85 ms · a card on the list with the app, never joined 49 → 198 ms (it now gets its streak) · a pass 199 → 205 ms · a repeat 24 → 30 ms · staff's Check in 168 → 196 ms · the 5-second poll 14.9 → 14.9 ms · day list 28.4 → 28.6 ms · Overview 53 → 54 ms. A check-in with an account is mostly the streak's recompute, as before.
- **Both re-checks closed their rounds** (*"Nothing Critical or High is open"*), each run on the real api and Redis, the integrity one on two processes. Their Lows fixed without a re-check: a marked key that is no longer a desk is counted by its address (its test RED with the rule broken); a read that slipped past a pause as it began is refused; a used pass's read gives its own count back; "New link" under a taken name has its own sentence. CI's first run failed on the new test file reading its settings with no database; fixed.
- **Kd's click-through: passed. Merged on his word** (*"merge"*, PR #162, all six checks green); ROADMAP ticked. From it, two asks, both *"yes"*: job 16f (the desk reads a pass in poor light, and plays a sound for each answer).
- **Open:** 16f next. A person whose record was first linked to them mid-week still counts as two on Overview's week figure for that week (the integrity re-check, read not run; the day's join does not reach earlier days). A NEW desk cannot be set up while its address's app-wide 600 a minute is being used up (desks already set up keep working). `tools/mutate-orgs.mjs` has two anchors on the visitors counts that have matched nothing since 16b-ii. The desk's camera reads poorly in dim light (Kd, 2026-10-03): put to him as its own job. Still open from Stage 1 item 10: a lapsed gym's desk keeps checking people in. Local Iron House holds Maya Patel 1003, Priya Nair 1004, Sofia Khan 1005 and a "Second desk" for the click-through.

## 2026-10-03 · 19a-ii (Folder B): Workout days, the leaderboard's third board (branch `leaderboard-workout-days`)

- **Why this job:** next in Folder B after 19a-i. **Risky (other people's names on a screen; a rule that ranks), Opus xhigh recommended; Kd: *"go"*.** No migration, no new package, no new cost.
- **Built:** the same routes take `board=workout_days` (five periods). `leaderboard/workouts.ts` is the ONE rule: each workout's gym day and the first reason it does not count (still ahead · before joining · no set · saved more than 7 days after it started); the board and "what counted" both read it. A profile lists Workout days too, and at a gym that checks nobody in it lists only that. Member web: the tab **Gym days · Workout days · Streak**, the period choice, ⓘ, and "what counted" with each workout's time, who counted it, and "Didn't count" with why.
- **Worst thing, first test:** a hidden member (Hide me, under 18 by age or record, taken off, no name) shows to nobody on the new board, any period, in a row, a profile, a count or a gap; and again at a gym that checks nobody in. **Breaks, each RED, restored sha256-identical:** the workout board ranked without the hide facts; a hidden person's profile answered where the gym checks nobody in; the board's count including the hidden.
- **Verified:** new table suite 8/8 (13 kinds of workout; day edges in six zones and on ten clock-change days, each checked against Intl; periods in three zones; two gyms; a rejoin; ties); routes 15/15; the model 3/3 (1,000 random gyms, 8,489 boards equal to a plain model, 357 s alone; Workout days on every second gym, since with every gym the full run took 721 s). Web 22/22 in the two leaderboard files. tsc 0 on shared and api; eslint 0 on api (`src test tools`) and the touched web files; shared 278/278. Full api run: 165 of 173 files; the 8 others were 5 s time-outs under load (`orgs.routes`, `checkin.routes`, four `memberList.*`, `leads.sentForYou`, `auth.signupBurst`), then 314/314 alone. Full web run: 141 of 142 files, the one `poseAssets.contract` (local Node 24).
- **Cost at full size** (`tools/measure-leaderboard-cost.ts`, 2,592 MHz, 5 runs): at 2,100 members with 689,626 workouts over 3 years, Workout days takes median 74–245 ms for the week and month boards and 1,926 ms all time; the server's thread is busy at most 65 ms on a board and 144 ms on a profile (three boards); "what counted" 39 ms. At 200 members (65,787 workouts): 27–30 ms the week and month boards, 235 ms all time, profile 383 ms. No index added. The older boards read slower in this run than in 19a-i's (Streak 3,934 ms, "On a roll" 6,178 ms at 2,100), cause unverified (the same code; a database now holding the workouts too).
- **Local data:** Iron Temple (`aihg_b`) has four more demo members with workouts and four demo workouts for Kd's account. My first seed wrongly removed the 19a-i demo accounts' memberships; put back from their visits (joined 1 Aug; any record link they had is lost).
- **Round one: 2 High, 4 Low, 3 weak tests, all fixed; each new test RED on the old code.** H1 a workout saved before it started counted once its date came (the reviewer synced 30 future-dated workouts in one sitting and took 1st): one that reached the server more than an hour before its own start never counts ("saved before its own start time"); the hour is for a phone's fast clock. H2 at a gym that checks nobody in the board opened on Gym days saying "there's no board": it opens on Workout days, and Gym days says where the board is. Lows: a set needs a rep or a hold; "Saved more than N days after"; an unknown reason reads "Didn't count"; list keys. Tests: the profile there asked for all time (RED with the guard removed); saved-early cases read after their date; the model's generator saves early too (8,489 boards agree, 418 s).
- **The re-check closed all nine and the round** (*"Nothing Critical or High is open. The round ends here."*); the reviewer's cheat run again counted 0 days. Its one Low fixed without a re-check: the no-check-in line reads "Workout days doesn't need the front desk — see that tab."
- **Merged on Kd's word** (*"merge"*, PR #163, six checks green on 90404c1 after one re-run: `auth.signupBurst`'s set-up hook timed out at 10 s with all 4,908 tests passing); ROADMAP ticked. Kd's click-through was not reported. **Next in Folder B:** 19a-iii.

## 2026-10-03 · 19a-i (Folder B): the members' leaderboard, Gym days and Streak (branch `leaderboard-members`)

- **Why this job:** next in Folder B. **Risky (other people's names on a screen; a rule that ranks), Opus xhigh recommended (Kd was on high); Kd: *"go"*, adding that a member of two gyms sees both boards (RULINGS 2026-10-03: the gym picker's tabs).** Migration `0065_leaderboard_show_me` (an under-18's Show me, and the boards' index; renumbered when 16b-ii's 0064 merged first); no new package, no new cost.
- **Built:** `GET /v1/orgs/:gymId/leaderboard` (Gym days, five periods; Streak), `/leaderboard/mine` (what counted), `/leaderboard/people/:userId` (a profile), `GET/PUT /v1/users/me/leaderboard` (Hide me). `leaderboard/visits.ts` holds the ONE rule for whose visit it is (16b-ii uses it) and the streak; "On a roll" now reads that streak. Member web: My Gyms' gym picker (tabs, remembered), the board under each gym (tabs, period, dates, ⓘ, circles, own row, what counted, profile, Hide me, refresh every minute), Hide me in Settings → Gym.
- **Worst thing, first test:** a hidden member (Hide me, under 18 by age or record, taken off, no name) shows to nobody, on any board or period, in a row, a profile, a count or a gap. **Breaks, each RED, restored sha256-identical:** hidden not filtered; places given before hiding (a gap); a hidden person's profile answered.
- **Verified:** new suites 8 route, 32 rank/names/periods, 3 model (1,000 random gyms in six zones and 29 Feb 2028, 17,094 boards equal to a plain model, first run with three viewers a gym; now one); cheers + nudges + overview 60/60 after their fixtures moved from the old tap to staff check-ins; api full run 4,871 of 4,873 (`leads.emailReports`, `leads.sentForYou` green alone; `auth.signupBurst`'s 200-person test 168 s alone against its 180 s limit, this machine); web 3,545 of 3,547 then green alone after relinking (`jsqr`), plus `poseAssets.contract` (local Node 24); tsc and eslint 0 on every touched file (Settings' 4 old errors are not this job's).
- **Cost at full size** (`tools/measure-leaderboard-cost.ts`, 2,592 MHz, 5 runs, half the members on a record and a fifth of their visits by record only; re-measured after round one): at 2,100 members and 690,039 visits over 3 years the server's thread is busy at most 82 ms on any read; the database takes median 49–79 ms for the week and month boards, 1,093 ms all time, 1,896 ms the Streak, 1,533 ms a profile, 2,862 ms "On a roll". At 200 members (65,621 visits): 12–14 ms the week and month boards, 83 ms all time, 116 ms the Streak, 143 ms "On a roll". Index `gym_attendance_counted_idx` (in 0065) lets those reads use the index alone.
- **Found and fixed on the way:** headless Edge on :3001/:5174 showed "1 more gym day to reach 3rd" for a 4th behind two tied 2nd (the server now sends the next place; test RED on the old line); a slow answer for one tab replaced the newer tab's board (test RED first); a 16b-i test dated 2 Oct read "expired" from 3 Oct and failed every run (its pattern now takes the date).
- **Round one: 3 High, 7 Low, 7 weak tests, all fixed, each new test RED with the fix taken out (eight breaks, restored sha256-identical).** H1 an under-18 by ONE gym's record was ranked at their other gym while their switch said hidden: one SQL rule for the boards and the switch (age given, or a date of birth on any live membership's record). H2 "On a roll" never ended at a gym that stopped checking in: nobody is on a roll, and no member has a streak, once the gym has counted no visit for 30 days. H3 "On a roll" re-read the gym's history for every member (the reviewer: 2.2 s at 200, 231 s at 2,100): read once, plus the index; now 143 ms and 2.9 s. Lows: no profile while the gym shows no board; a name typed as an address is no name; "Add your full name"; staff with no typed name read "staff"; `orgs/repo.ts`'s doubled line ends put back (its diff is 40 added, 75 removed again); sheets close by their X only; no refresh in a hidden tab. Routes 14/14, ranking 33/33, model 3/3 (5,994 boards), cheers + nudges + overview + migration + export 114 in all; web 251/251 in the touched area; tsc and eslint 0. Kd asked why the member screen is on the web: it is the test rig of RULINGS 2026-09-21 until the phone app exists; his answer on keeping it is still open. **The re-check closed all ten and the round** (*"Nothing Critical or High is open. The round ends here."*). Its one Low, recorded: "On a roll" measured 143 ms at 200 members and 2,862 ms at 2,100 in the builder's run, 672 ms and 1,919 ms in the reviewer's, same tool and clock; the range is the number. **Merged on Kd's word (PR #160, 2026-10-03)**; ROADMAP ticked. Kd's click-through was not finished (he stopped at sign-in to ask why the member screen is on the web). **Next in Folder B:** 19a-ii. **Found in 16b-ii (Folder A), not changed here:** Attendance and Overview count a person as their account OR their record, so someone who used a key tag and their pass on one day is two people there; the fix is `visitsWithOwner`'s owner in that count.

## 2026-10-02 · 19a re-planned (Folder B): the leaderboard, for accuracy (branch `leaderboard-members`)

- **Why:** 19a was next in Folder B. Kd judged its plan too thin (*"people will get angry and feel bad if the leaderboard is not accurate and tansparent"*), gave the chat a free hand, and noted that some gyms use only the member features. Planning only: no code.
- **Read and measured:** PushPress, Wodify, Strava, Myzone, F45, Fitbit, the UK children's code, Duolingo (2026-10-02). In the repo: the camera counts 3 of 58 exercises (`aihg_b`); a visit made before the app carries only `entry_id`, while every reader keys on `user_id`; "On a roll" has its own weekly streak; runs still go to the old server; nothing deletes or back-dates a visit.

## 2026-10-02 · Stage 4 item 11 (Folder B): the api talks to Postgres on ten connections (branch `db-pool`)

- **Why this job:** next in Folder B (19a–19d wait on Folder A's 16a and 17c, 19b on photo storage). **Risky (it changes how every request runs beside another, sign-in and money included), Opus xhigh; Kd: *"go"*.** No migration, no new package, no new cost.
- **Built:** `DATABASE_POOL_MAX` (1–50, default 10) opens the api's pool (was `max: 1`). Neon's smallest compute allows 104 connections and its pooler 10,000 clients (Neon's connection-pooling page, read 2026-10-02); the worker keeps its 2. With one connection every transaction ran alone in its process, so all 128 transactions were audited for a check that only that made safe: four were, all fixed. One trial per OWNER takes an owner lock (two gyms pressed at once gave two trials); My Gyms reads its two lists in one REPEATABLE READ snapshot; a new one-time token locks its user (two at once left two live links); a first streak takes an advisory lock (no row to lock yet). Old "the pool is one connection" comments and spec Part 3's rule reworded.
- **Worst thing, first test:** two people tap Join at the same instant on ONE server for a gym's last place: one gets it, one is told the gym is full. **Breaks, each RED, restored sha256-identical:** the pool back to 1 (`/health` stuck behind a waiting sign-in), the gym's row lock removed (both joined). The four fixes removed: two trials, two live links, the streak not waiting, My Gyms showing the gym in neither list 11 times in 80.
- **Verified:** api **4,705 of 4,706** in one full run on `aihg_b` (the one, `leads.sentForYou`'s whole-app day count, the known full-run flake; the file then 18/18 alone); new tests 8/8; tsc and eslint 0 (api).
- **Cost** (`tools/measure-pool-cost.ts`, 10,000 leads, mains 2,592 MHz, 3 runs each): four staff opening the Delete box for all, a bystander's request waited median 21–26 ms, worst 289–425 ms on one connection; median 2.2–2.8 ms, worst 93–133 ms on ten.
- **Found, older than this job, on ROADMAP's known defects with their fixes:** the archive sweep can archive a gym paying at that instant and delete its list; a lead's "may have gone" mark skips the Send-for-me switch; the lead sender and a lead's deletion lock in opposite orders.
- **Round one: 1 High, 3 Low, 1 weak test, all fixed, each new test RED on the old code.** H1 a refresh in flight survived log out, log out everywhere and a password reset (the reviewer's run: 8 resets in 20): revoking now locks the user first, waiting out a half-done rotation (two tests hold a refresh half way). L1 an account deleted mid-Remove (all, selected, or an import's leavers) answered 500: the press now rolls back and runs once more, answering `list_changed` (`onceMore.ts`). L2 "staff removed" written twice: only when the row is deleted. L3 a twin photo's file removed after the commit, not under the gym's lock. The trial test now holds both presses at the gym's lock, three owners. api 4,706 of 4,710 in one full run (my new test reused another's user name, renamed; `orgs.routes` two 5 s timeouts under load and the scanner's 503, each file green alone); the six touched files 102/102; tsc and eslint 0. **The re-check closed the round** (*"Nothing Critical or High is open: the round ends here."*; its race: 0 of 40 resets). All six checks green. **Merged on Kd's word (PR #156, 2026-10-02)**; ROADMAP ticked. **Next in Folder B:** 19a (16a merged the same day, PR #155).

## 2026-10-02 · 16a (Folder A): the check-in scan, on the server (branch `checkin-scan-server`)

- **Why this job:** next in Folder A after 3d. **Risky (other people's data, a signed pass), Opus xhigh; Kd: *"go"*** after asking, in plain words, how the desk works, whether gyms use it (PushPress, PureGym, Mindbody, Zen Planner, Wodify checked that day), whether a web page is less safe than an app, and whether a pass can check somebody in at the wrong gym. Migration `0063_checkin_desk`; no new package; new optional setting `CHECKIN_PASS_SECRET` (production without it: passes off, key tags still work).
- **Built:** `GET /v1/users/me/checkin-pass` (one pass a person, RULINGS 2026-09-23: 62 letters, HMAC-signed, 30-second window and the next, used once via Redis); Settings' device routes under `/v1/orgs/:gymId/checkin-devices` (`org.manage`; add with a one-hour one-time link, new link, switch off — off works on a lapsed plan; 20 a gym); `POST /v1/checkin/device/claim` sets an httpOnly cookie for `/v1/checkin` only; `POST /v1/checkin/scan` reads a pass or a key tag (member number). One pure rule `checkin/scanRule.ts`. A visit hangs on the record (`entry_id`), with `user_id` when the person has the app; a listed person not joined in the app is found by proved email; a household picks by name, else "see staff". Joining two records moves visits; deleting a record keeps an app member's visits.
- **Worst thing, first tests:** a stranger's, spliced, old, used or foreign pass never checks anybody in; a desk's key gets the same answer as nobody on every other route of the app (swept from Fastify's own route table). **Breaks, each RED, restored sha256-identical:** the pass's signature unchecked; a pass usable twice; another gym's list admitting (stopped by the new foreign key, a 500); a former record admitting.
- **Verified:** api **4,788 of 4,789** in one full run (the one, `leads.sentForYou`'s whole-app day count, the known full-run flake; the file then 18/18 alone); new suites 26/26 route, 90/90 unit; tsc and eslint 0 (api, shared); shared 278/278. `memberList.byHand`'s reference census and `db.migration`'s attendance constraints updated for the new columns.
- **Cost at full size** (`.cost/cost-16a.ts`, 2,592 MHz, 10,000 records, 200 app members, two runs): a pass scan median 396–412 ms (worst 598–979), of which the existing streak-and-badge step is ~150–290 ms (each commit ~25–30 ms on this machine's Docker); a key tag 91 ms; a repeat 52–55 ms; a red 22–24 ms; a pass 10 ms; the person in 20 gyms 285 ms (re-measured after round one: the first script timed refusals).
- **Round one: 2 High, 2 Low, 1 weak test.** High 1: a gym closing deleted its app members' desk visits (streak days) — the archive's list delete now lets go of the record first (test RED first). High 2: anyone at the tablet could type member numbers and read names with payment words — **Kd chose the fix** (RULINGS 2026-10-02): 20 key tags a minute a device, 10 unknown numbers pause key tags 10 minutes, shown on the device list; three breaks RED; api 4,816 of 4,817 after it (the same `leads.sentForYou` flake, 18/18 alone). Lows: nightly numbers count app visits only; cost script re-measured. **The re-check closed the round** (both Highs run on the real api and Redis); its one Low, a comment's width, fixed without a re-check.
- **Open:** 16b builds the desk page, staff check-in and the live log, and makes the Attendance page and Overview count visits by record. The two extra passes run once over 16a–16c before any gym uses check-in.

## 2026-10-02 · Stage 4 item 10 (Folder B): a whole gym signs up on one internet address (branch `signup-burst`)

- **Why this job:** 19a, 19c and 19d wait on Folder A's 16a and 17c; 19b on photo storage. **Risky (sign-in), Opus xhigh; Kd: *"ok"*** after asking whether a hacker with many accounts gains (no; one hole closed by the check: *"yes add"*). No migration, no new package, no new cost (Turnstile is free; its keys already set for the gym page).

Checking a code 1,000 an address, Google 600. A person's own limits unchanged. Password routes (no screen uses them) keep 20. The app-wide floor counts a signed-in person against themselves and the rest against the address (600 a minute each, Folder A's 3d numbers taken at the merge, which kept this job's robot check as 3d's record asks); the token is checked once a request (`accessClaimsOf`); browsers keep the CORS pre-check 2 hours. Shared: `robotCheck.ts` (was in `gymPage.ts`: a load-order loop with `auth.ts`).

- **Verified:** api **4,654 of 4,654** in one full run on `aihg_b` (first run 4,652: `leads.sentForYou`'s known flake, and `leads.routes`' 300 presses in a minute meeting the per-person floor — the test now fills the Leads count directly); new suite 12/12; shared 278/278; web 3,421/3,421 (`poseAssets.contract` local Node 24); tsc and eslint 0 (api, shared, the changed web files; `AuthContext.jsx` has 5 `only-export-components` errors on master too).
- **Cost:** reads no list of people. The token check is 0.11 ms a request (3 runs of 100,000, mains 2,592 MHz, `.cost/cost-10.mts`); signed-in routes already made it and now reuse it.
- **Kd's click-through: passed** (kautilyadb@gmail.com at a busy address: the check passed unseen, the code came by itself, landed on Create your gym).
- **Round one: 1 High, 4 Low, 3 weak tests, all fixed, each new test RED on the old code.** H1 20 empty requests and 30 made-up answers locked a whole address out of codes for an hour: failures are no longer counted against the address. L2 a request that sends nothing no longer spends the free 20. L4 with our keys a reply must name its form (`sign_in` / `enquiry`). L3 a comment. A real-Redis case added. IPv6 /64 noted on Stage 4 item 1. api **4,666 of 4,666**; shared 278/278. **The re-check closed those and found 2 High:** R1 500 empty sends filled the address's 500 (the gate now answers them 400 itself); 1,000 junk code checks locked an address for an hour (the address now counts only wrong guesses at live codes, `WrongGuessError`). The same class at Google fixed too: only answers Google refused count, 60 a minute. Each test RED first; breaks B5–B7 RED, restored identical. api 4,668 of 4,669, then **4,671 of 4,671** after the guess fix, in one run (`orgs.routes` hook timeout under load and `leads.sentForYou`: 171/171 alone). **The second re-check closed both** and found on master too that a code took as many guesses as were sent at once (15 at once → 15 tries); Kd: *"yes"*, fixed here: the try is taken first (`takeAttempt`, `attempts < 5` in one statement), then compared; the address is charged only for a granted try. RED first (15 → 5); harness A3 split (A3 the kill, A3b the try count, both RED); A6 alive by design (two walls). **Its re-check closed the round** (*"Nothing Critical or High is open: the round ends here."*); its Low, a stale comment, removed. All six checks green. **Merged on Kd's word (PR #153, 2026-10-02)**; ROADMAP ticked. ## 2026-10-02 · 3d (Folder A): the two extra passes over "getting in", and their fixes (branch `getting-in-fixes`)

- **Why this job:** next in Folder A after 3c. The passes ran in two fresh terminals (`reviews/getting-in-security.md`, `-integrity.md`): 8 High, 2 Low, no Critical. **Risky (sign-in, other people's data, emails), Opus xhigh; Kd: *"go"*.** No migration, no new package, no new cost.
- **Fixed:** an Invite press writes every batch under the gym's lock and only for addresses a current record holds; the senders mark "may have gone" in one statement that checks the invitation is still open, the person still listed and not unsubscribed, the gym not stopped (member and staff emails); Join and Put back hold the account, so a deletion at that moment is seen or closes what they opened; Staff appoints at once only an account that proved its address; matching a file is linear on a shared key; a moved-list preview is compared once per list version; the whole-table ANALYZE left the request path for a five-minute worker check (1,000 rows changed); the app-wide floor counts a signed-in person on their own (600 a minute) and answers a typed `rate_limited` with its wait; sign-in codes 300 an hour an address (was 20); "Locker combo", "Memorable word", "Maiden name" never kept; a re-sent staff invitation kept through its new 7 days.
- **Worst thing, first tests:** somebody taken off between two batches of a press, or while the worker asks the mail domain, is never invited or emailed. **Breaks, each RED, restored sha256-identical:** the batch's listed-address check, the member sender's guarded mark, the account lock in Join, the hold in Put back, the proof in Staff, the staff sender's guarded mark, the tidy-up's new date. Never-keep, matching (26 s → under 1 s at 20,000), the floor, the code door and the preview memo each RED on the old code.
- **Verified:** api **4,672 of 4,673** in one full run (the one, `leads.sentForYou`'s whole-app day count, the known full-run flake; the file then 18/18 alone); new suites green; tsc and eslint 0 (api). 82 staff appointments in 28 old suites now prove the address first (`test/proveAddress.ts`), as every member has since 3c. A leftover pending Resend report from the security reviewer's run was removed from Folder A's local database.
- **Cost at full size** (`.cost/cost-getting-in.ts`, 2,592 MHz, 2 runs): crafted 10,000-row file, preview's bystander worst 474–706 ms (reviewer: 1,538–2,585); moved-list reads 684–692 ms once, then 67–111 ms (was 1,069–1,537 each); one-row confirm 116–295 ms (was 1,150–1,259). A 10,000-row confirm is still ~2.9 s, as recorded in §9.9.
- **Both re-checks closed the round** (each re-ran its races and measurements on the fix). The security re-check's one Low: one address can now spend the day's 5,000 sign-in emails in about 17 hours; Folder B's open `signup-burst` puts a robot check (`codeRobotGate`) in front of the same limiter, so **whichever of the two merges second keeps that check**. Locally, the `aihg-dev-worker-1` container runs older code on Redis 0 and fails the new statistics job as unknown until it is rebuilt.

Member web: `/org/join` → `/invitations`; Settings and setup's "Your gym" show the invitations; no kept code in sign-in, the Google return or setup.

Web 3,398/3,398 (`poseAssets.contract` local Node 24). Shared 278/278. tsc 0 (api, shared); eslint 0 (api, changed web files; member `Settings.jsx` has 4 errors on master too). gitleaks clean. Worker run on local Redis: `bull:rollups:repeat:orgs.join_sweep` 1 → 0.
- **Cost at full size:** reads no list of people; a retired route answers before authentication and any database read.
- **Kd's click-through on Harbour Gym: all passed.** **Round one: 1 High, 5 Low, 1 weak test, all fixed (df372ff).** High: My Gyms' empty state still said to enter a code (now the invitation; test RED first). The worker's two decisions moved to `joinSweepSchedule.ts`, tested on real Redis and Postgres, each RED when broken. **The re-check closed the round**; its Low ("See the member list" → "See who's in the app") fixed without a re-check. **Next:** the two extra passes over all of "getting in" (spec §10.7) before any gym uses it.

## 2026-10-02 · 20c-vii (Folder B): delete many leads at once (branch `leads-delete-many`)

- **Why this job:** Stage 4 item 1 was next; Kd ruled going online waits until the features are built (RULINGS 2026-10-02), so it and Stage 4 item 4 moved to the end of Folder B's list. 19a–19d wait on Folder A's 16a and 17c, so 20c-vii. **Risky (other people's data), Opus xhigh; Kd: *"go"*.** No migration, no new package, no new cost.
- **Built:** Leads gets a tick box on every row and the heading, Gmail's "Select all 132 leads" line, and a bar "N selected · Delete · Clear" (top on a computer, above the tabs on a phone). Delete opens a box naming every lead that goes (five names, "and N more · See all") and what goes with them. `POST …/leads/selection`, `…/selected/delete-preview`, `…/selected/delete`, the member list's pattern (§18.5): ticked ids or a filter with its count and digest; the press re-reads the ids under the gym's lock and deletes only if they are exactly the box's (409 `leads_changed` with the new box, `selection_changed` for a Select all that moved); the same press twice answers `alreadyDeleted`. One log line per lead and one for the press. The list, its total and Select all share one filter (`filterCondition`). A lapsed gym gets no tick boxes.
- **Worst thing, first tests:** a lead nobody ticked, or another gym's, is never deleted. **Breaks, each RED, restored sha256-identical:** the gym left out of the box's and the press's reads (twice, before and after the press was slimmed), Select all ignoring the status, the press not checking the box's digest (twice).
- **Verified:** api **4,634 of 4,635** in one full run on `aihg_b` (the one, `leads.sentForYou`'s whole-app day count, the known full-run flake; the five leads suites then 75/75 alone); new suite 8/8; shared 278/278; web 3,391/3,392 in one run (`gymPageSheet` 18/18 alone; `poseAssets.contract` local Node 24) plus the new view tests 23/23; tsc and eslint 0 (api, shared, the changed web files). Seen in headless Edge at 1280 and 390 (`.cost/look-20c-vii.mjs`).
- **Cost at full size** (2,592 MHz, `tools/measure-leads-delete-cost.ts`, setImmediate probe, 6 runs): 10,000 leads, answering nobody at most: a page 2.2 ms, Select all 4.5 ms, the box for all 12.2 ms, Delete all 16.1 ms, the box and Delete for 500 ticked 1.6 and 3.2 ms. At 200: under 5 ms (one 31.7 ms run in six). First shape built 10,000 log rows in the server (59 ms); now the database writes them in the delete's own statement.
- **Kd's click-through on frfgrgrg: *"all passed"*.** He asked what stops a flood of form messages from many emails: answered from 20c-iv-a's limits (robot check, 60 an hour an address, 120 an hour a page, 1,000 New from the page).
- **Round one: 1 High, 2 Low, 3 weak tests, all fixed, each new test RED on the old code.** H1 a Select all press sent again (a lost answer) said "nothing was done" after deleting everyone: the press now runs under the gym's lock and recognises its own logged digest. L1 a press whose box named nobody answered "0 deleted" and logged it: now the box again, nothing logged. L2 the filter was read before the lock: now after it (two connections: a lead moved to Contacted while the press waits stays). Tests: a same-count swap (RED with the digest check removed), the box re-read after `selection_changed`, unticking one after Select all (both RED when broken). Delete suite 12/12, leads 23/23, Delete screen 9/9. Delete all 10,000 now answers nobody at most 25.9 ms (6 runs, 12–26).
- **The re-check closed the round** (*"Nothing Critical or High is open. The round ends here."*; it re-ran H1 on a second api with the fix). All six checks green. **Merged on Kd's word (PR #151, 2026-10-02)**; ROADMAP ticked. **Next in Folder B:** 19a waits on Folder A's 16a, 19c on 17c; the first job whose needs are met.

## 2026-10-01 · 1d-iii-b (Folder B): a smaller size through Razorpay (branch `razorpay-smaller-size`)

- **Why this job:** next on Folder B's list. **Risky (money), Opus xhigh; Kd: *"go"*.** His one question answered *"A"*: a gym with too many members on the day STAYS on its size (RULINGS 2026-10-01). Migration `0061_razorpay_smaller_size`; no new package, no new cost.

- **Built:** smaller rows in Change size for Razorpay plans (Continue to Razorpay, the ₹5 line). Approved, it waits on the plan (`pending_subscription_ref`); 3 h before the paid month ends the worker counts members: fit → the new plan takes the old one's place and the old is cancelled at Razorpay (a later charge of it refunded); too many → the new plan is cancelled, the gym stays and is emailed. Cancel this change, Cancel plan, a second smaller size, a bigger size, a withdrawn mandate and a missed day each end the waiting plan (`strandedSmallerSizes`). The 3-day email and the last days' box say it stays, and offer "Move to 500" through Razorpay's window.
- **Worst thing, first tests:** one month never charged twice; too many members → nobody removed, size and price kept. **Breaks:** members never counted, size taken at approval, decision never applied — each RED, restored sha256-identical; "old plan never cancelled" stayed green (two other paths cancel it).

Real test-mode run on :3001: ₹12,500 plan → 200 approved → worker at the day: old plan `cancelled` at Razorpay, ₹7,500 plan waiting to charge; Cancel this change cancelled a second one there. Seen in headless Edge at 1000 and 390.
- **Cost at full size:** reads no list of people; one member count per gym per month, the count Paddle's smaller size already runs.
- **Kd's click-through on frfgrgrg: all passed** (after its 1d-iii-a checkout row, wiped when that job re-added 0060's columns by hand on `aihg_b`, was restored from Razorpay's record). His two asks, built: Cancel this change asks first; the plan screens' words cut short (RULINGS 2026-10-01).
- **Round one: 3 High, 3 Low, all fixed (85142af), each with a test run red first.** H1 the browser's sync and Razorpay's webhook for one approval cancelled it: approval answers "already". H2 a plan that renewed before its day kept the big price: now decided as any day, the renewal refunded when the members fit. H3 "₹5" is a card's only: "a small charge". **Re-check closed the round**; its two Lows (an indent, "may check") fixed. Kd's re-click: *"all passed"*.
- **Merged on Kd's word (PR #148, 2026-10-01)**; ROADMAP ticked. **Next in Folder B:** the 1d-iii feature's two extra passes (security, integrity) before any real gym pays, then Stage 4 item 1. `aihg_b` has 0061; Folder A's database needs it migrated.

## 2026-10-01 · 1d-iii-a (Folder B): a bigger size through Razorpay (branch `razorpay-bigger-size`, PR #146)

- **Why this job:** next on Folder B's list. 1d-iii split in two (ROADMAP): this is the bigger size; 1d-iii-b, the smaller, is next. **Risky (money), Opus xhigh; Kd: *"go"*.** Migration `0060_razorpay_bigger_size` (renumbered after Folder A's 0059 merged); no new package, no new cost.
- **Measured on the Razorpay test account first:** `PATCH /subscriptions/:id` on a card mandate is refused (*"Can't update subscription immediately when card mandate is applicable"*), so a new size is a new subscription; one made with `start_at` + an add-on shows "₹X will be charged now", and once paid is `authenticated`, `paid_count` 0, its add-on invoice `paid`.
- **Built:** Change size for a paying Razorpay plan. Bigger: the rest of this month's difference priced by our server (`upgradeCharge`, table-tested), a new subscription from the paid month's end with that add-on and a 30-minute window; once Razorpay's invoice says paid it replaces the old plan under the gym's lock, the old one is cancelled at Razorpay (worker retries) and any later charge of it refunded. Smaller rows say "Not available yet".
- **Worst thing, first test:** the bigger size opens only once Razorpay says it was paid, and the old plan never charges again (refunded if it does). **Breaks, each RED, restored identical:** the add-on not checked as paid, the old plan not cancelled, a replaced plan's later events ignored.
- **Real run (api on :3001 with the test keys):** ₹7,500 plan paid, then 500 members: ₹4,999.92 taken now, ₹12,500 from 1 Nov, old plan `cancelled` at Razorpay, no warnings in the log.
- **Kd's click-through on frfgrgrg: all pass.** He found the card's buttons badly laid out; they wait for R4 (RULINGS 2026-10-01, *"wait for R4"*).
- **Round one: 1 Critical, 1 High, 1 Low, 3 untested guards, all fixed (332a1ac).** C1 a paid window closed by a newer press kept the money: now refunded, its page says so. H1 a second bigger size in one month was refused: priced from that checkout's own month (`period_start`, added to the migration; on `aihg_b` by hand). L1 a code's name. The same-month and `active` guards each turn the new test red when broken. **Re-check closed the round.**
- **Verified:** api **4,550 of 4,550** on `aihg_b`; web 3,355/3,355 (`poseAssets.contract` local Node 24); shared 278/278; tsc and eslint 0.
- **Merged on Kd's word (PR #146, 2026-10-01)**; ROADMAP ticked. **Next in Folder B: 1d-iii-b.** The feature's two extra passes (security, integrity) run once after 1d-iii-b, before any real gym pays. Folder A's database needs 0060 migrated; `aihg_b` needs Folder A's 0059.

## 2026-10-01 · 1d-ii (Folder B): a Razorpay plan paid, its card changed, cancelled, from the console (branch `razorpay-manage-plan`)

- **Why this job:** next on Folder B's list after 1e. **Risky (money), Opus xhigh; Kd: *"go"*.** Migration `0058_razorpay_cancel` (`subscriptions.cancel_sent_at`, refund reason `cancelled`); no new package, no new cost.
- **Measured on Razorpay's test account first (a throwaway subscription paid with its recurring test card):** a month-end cancel leaves Razorpay's record exactly as before (`active`, next charge set, nothing scheduled), cannot be taken back (*"No Pending update for this subscription"*), answers 200 when asked again, and is refused before a first charge; the change-card window (₹5 check) leaves the plan as it was and offers card or bank account.
- **Built:** Pay now (Razorpay's own page for the oldest bill owed; also in the overdue prompt), Update payment method (Razorpay's window in change mode), Cancel plan with a box (the end date, nothing more charged, "Your N members keep everything until then"), Keep my plan. The cancel is kept by us and sent 3 h before the paid month ends (`PLAN_CANCEL_DECIDE_HOURS`); a paid trial's is cancelled at once then and kept live to its end; an overdue plan's ends now. A month Razorpay charges after a cancel ends the plan at its paid end and is refunded (`razorpayCancelOutcome`); a plan with a failed payment counts as paid only when its newest invoice is paid (`razorpayOwes`). `invoice.paid` webhooks kept; the runbook asks for them.
- **Worst thing, first test:** a cancel never ends a plan before the month paid for ends, and only this gym's billing staff can cancel, pay or change it. **Breaks, each RED, restored sha256-identical:** a Razorpay-ended plan ended at once (4 table rows + the trial test), the cancel sent the moment it is pressed, the worker cancelling now instead of at the month's end.
- **Also met:** `fakePaddle`'s fixed clock (2026-10-01T00:00Z) was overtaken by real time today, turning two Paddle paid-trial tests red on every branch; Folder A fixed it the same way first (c1f861d), and its version was kept when master was taken in.
- **Verified:** api **4,511 of 4,511** on `aihg_b` (rule table 59/59, Razorpay suite 35/35, Paddle 57/57); shared 274/274; web 3,338/3,338 (`poseAssets.contract` local Node 24); tsc and eslint 0. The real adapter run against the test account matched the fake. Seen in headless Edge on frfgrgrg: the box, the set-to-end card, Keep my plan (left as it was).
- **Not reachable on the test account:** a real failed payment (Razorpay retries over 3 days), so Pay now's happy path is proven on the fake only.
- **Kd's click-through: *"ok pass"*** after two fixes it found: the box's "and get the free app only after" cut (*"not needed"*), and its Go back is now Keep my plan.
- **Round one: 2 High, 2 Low, all fixed, each test RED without its fix, restored identical.** H1 a halted plan paid from its bill's page stayed active free: now paid only through the month that bill covers (its `billing_end`, seen on the test account), and the worker re-reads live plans past their month's end (once a quarter hour). H2 a payment after a cancel went unrefunded once Razorpay said `cancelled`, or when a retry landed between Cancel and the cancel: refunded whatever Razorpay says, from the earliest of the paid end, the press and the write, to the second. L1 Razorpay refuses to cancel a cancelled plan (*"Subscription is not cancellable in cancelled status."*, tried): read back and counted done; the fake refuses it too. L2 the schema index's quotes.
- **Re-check: round one closed; N1 High, N2 and N3 Low, from the fixes, all fixed, N1/N2 RED without, restored identical.** N1 a plan Razorpay stopped charging, its paid month over and no bill issued: Pay now now says the last payment covered up to that date and to press Update payment method. N2 the "charged after cancel" error only when a refund is written. N3 the two billing suites hold one Postgres lock in turn (their worker sweeps every gym). CI also went red on a test whose first payment fell in the same second as its Cancel: that test's paid month now starts a day earlier.
- **The second re-check closed the round** (*"Nothing Critical/High is open: the round ends here."*); its Low (Pay now's new words for a bill paid a moment ago, still covering days ahead) fixed without a re-check, test RED first. **Verified:** api **4,521 of 4,521** on `aihg_b` before that Low; billing files 161/161 after it; tsc and eslint 0. **Merged on Kd's word (PR #144, 2026-10-01)**, all six checks green; ROADMAP ticked. **Next in Folder B: 1d-iii.** Folder A's database needs 0058 migrated.

- **Why this job:** next on Folder B's list after 2f-i (RULINGS 2026-09-30). **Risky** (money; a person's email sent to Paddle), Opus xhigh; Kd: *"go"*. No migration, no new package, no new cost.

Those countries get the email only (`paddleWindowCountry`); of the 24 a gym can choose, 16 get the country filled in.

- **Verified:** api 4,423 of 4,423 tests on `aihg_b`; `leads.routes` and `orgs.routes` hooks timed out under the full load and pass alone 176/176; billing Paddle 55/55; shared 273/273; web 3,306 of 3,306 (`poseAssets.contract` local Node 24); tsc 0 (api, shared); eslint 0 (api, shared, changed web files).

H2 an owner's stored email the console can't read back (a Google or migrated account) broke Subscribe: the server sends it only when `payerEmailSchema` passes, Paddle and Razorpay alike. L1 a comment's count. The country test now reads Paddle's own saved reply (`test/fixtures/paddle-countries.json`, 252 countries). Each new test red on the old code first; the list with the US taken off, red.

## 2026-09-30 · 20c-vi (Folder B): the two extra passes over Leads, fixed (branch `leads-safety-fixes`)

- **Why this job:** Leads (20c) was finished, so its two extra passes (security, data integrity) ran in fresh terminals before anybody uses it. **Risky** (a public form, other people's emails), Opus xhigh. Migration `0055_leads_safety`; no new package, no new cost.
- **Kd's decision (RULINGS 2026-09-30):** bounces and spam reports on the app's emails to people who asked on a gym's page count apart. They can pause only those emails, never the gym's invitations (Kd: *"yes"*).
- **Security, 2 High fixed:** (1) a stranger could stop a gym's emails with one form send and one spam click. Page emails are now counted apart, and once paused they go to staff's Email due. (2) the form could fill a gym to its lead limit. It now stops at 1,000 untouched page leads, so staff can always add.
- **Security, Lows fixed:** a greeted name keeps only letters, and look-alike dots count as dots. Limits are per page and per address: 60 an hour, and 600 per address across all pages. Robot-check failures are counted apart (30 per page and address), so a robot never spends a person's allowance. The 10-per-address share applies only once a page has taken 60 in the hour. Leads now says how many messages the page turned away, and when page emails are paused.
- **Integrity, 2 High fixed:** (1) the same email could be sent twice after a re-tick. A try in flight now stays the app's everywhere, backed by a partial unique index. (2) staff were told "send it yourself", then refused.
- **Integrity, Lows fixed:** a stale panel can't undo Joined (409 `lead_changed`); Add pressed again says "0 added" truly; the same message twice is kept once; a spam report after Stop is kept; the key fill checks the address it read. **N1**: a try still out stays the app's even when its address turns up on the member list.
- **Tests:** each fix's test went red first. N1 was broken by hand, went red, and was put back. **Both re-checks closed their rounds** ("Nothing Critical or High is open"). Their Lows were fixed without another re-check (§2.6). Low A (clearing a flood) is ROADMAP **20c-vii**.
- **Verified:** api 4,398 of 4,404 on `aihg_b`; the 6 are member-list upload tests that ran alongside the web suite, and their 2 files pass alone 59/59. gymPage 16/16. Web 3,288 of 3,289, plus 2 new render tests: `settingsHealth` passes alone 9/9, and `poseAssets.contract` is the local Node 24 file. Shared 273/273. tsc 0. eslint 0 on every changed file.
- **Merged on Kd's word (PR #139, 2026-09-30)**, all six checks green; ROADMAP ticked. **Open:** run `RUNBOOK/fill-lead-email-keys.md` once the new api is serving. Folder A's database needs 0053 and 0055 migrated. **Next in Folder B: 2f.**

H1 a join or a re-added past member changed values and left stale marks: `writeEntry` now requires the review, joins carry a filled field's marks, a revive drops those on changed fields. L3 the RULINGS line. **The re-check closed the round** (*"Nothing Critical or High is open. The round ends here."*); its one Low (a comment on the wrong function) fixed.

## 2026-09-27 · 20c-ii (Folder B): lead follow-up emails, sent by the gym (branch `lead-follow-ups`)

- **Why this job:** next on Folder B's list (1d waits on Razorpay; R4–R8 on Folder A). **Risky (other people's data), Opus xhigh; Kd said *"go"*.** Planned as emails the app sends; Kd refused anything adding to our email count (*"is there not a other way ? standard industry standard"*) and took the gym sending from its own email (RULINGS 2026-09-27). No new package; migration `0047_lead_follow_ups`.
- **Built:** `gym_leads` keeps `follow_ups_sent`, `follow_up_last_at` and `follow_up_due_on` (the gym's day), with a CHECK that only a New lead with the tick is ever due. `followUpDueOn` (pure): day of the tick, then +3 after the first is sent, +4 after the second. `POST …/leads/:id/follow-up {step}` (members.confirm, live plan, same press twice counts once). A new address starts them again; a status that moves stops them, New resumes. List: `?followUp=due`, `counts.followUpsDue`.
- **Screen:** the lead's panel says which email is due and when, what went, or why they stopped; **Email {first name}** is a `mailto:` with fixed words and the gym's address (else city); **Mark email N as sent**. The list has an **Email due** chip with its count and an Email due tag on the row.
- **Worst thing, first test:** nobody but a New lead who said yes is ever due (no tick, phone only, Contacted, On trial, Lost, a new address, untick, Joined, one sent then untick, one sent then Lost); the database refuses a due day otherwise; a stranger, rival, member, trainer and nobody signed in refused, nothing written. **Three breaks, each RED, restored sha256-identical:** the rule without the tick (unit red; the route test was green, so the sent-then-stopped cases were added, then red), without the status (both red), Mark as sent without the due check (red).
- **Verified:** api tsc 0 · eslint 0 · shared tsc 0 · lint 0 · 232/232 · the due rule 11/11 · follow-up routes 6/6 · leads routes 22/22 · **whole api suite on `aihg_b`: 3,825 of 3,828**, the 3 (db.migration's two 5 s timeouts, orgs' GBP clash) pass alone · web 2,965 of 2,965 (`poseAssets.contract` local Node 24) · web eslint 0 on every changed file.
- **Cost at the ceiling** (9,998 leads, 999 due, ON BATTERY at 1,495 MHz so an upper bound; bystander worst of 3): open Leads 26–38 ms, Email due 25 ms, its page 2 17–27 ms, with a search 14–18 ms, Mark as sent 48–86 ms, a status tap on a due lead 59–188 ms, add a ticked lead 32–80 ms.
- **Kd's click-through passed** (*"all pass"*); asked whether this is industry standard, told most gym software sends these itself, he chose "Send them for me" as its own job, 20c-v, at most 100 leads a gym a month on every plan (RULINGS 2026-09-27).
- **Round one: 2 High, 4 Low, 5 weak tests, all fixed.** H1 the gaps were shown but not enforced (all three marked in 120 ms): the server refuses a step before its day and the panel offers Email and Mark only when due. ## 2026-09-25 · Stage 3 item 1c-iii (Folder B): a smaller size (branch `paddle-smaller-size`)

- **Risky (money, Paddle's replies), Opus xhigh; Kd said *"go"*** to the end of the paid month over switching at once with a credit (RULINGS 2026-09-25). **Rebuilt after his click-through** (*"the gym already paid now we are downgrading instantly ?"*): the gym keeps its whole size until the date, its members are counted then; the Plan card redesigned with one Change size button. **Then** (*"are we doing injustice to gyms"*): too many members moves the gym to the smallest size they fit, and it stays only when none does; in the last 3 days billing staff are asked in a pop-up to remove members, move to that size, or stay (RULINGS, three lines that day).
- **Built**: migration `0045` (`subscriptions.pending_plan_id/pending_from/pending_held_at/pending_warned_at`, `billing_plan_changes.members_counted`). Choosing a smaller size on a paying plan only writes it down; the worker (`applyPendingSizes`), 3 h before the next charge, counts the members under the gym's lock: too many → the smallest smaller size they fit (`smallestFittingPlan`, `requested_plan_id` kept) is made instead, or if none the size is dropped; the count kept for the card, email; they fit → the smaller limit holds for joins from then (`pending_held_at`, in every limit read) and Paddle's price changes with `do_not_bill`; after a missed renewal, with proration (a credit). A paid trial moves at once and refuses too many. Keep is `DELETE …/billing/size/pending` ("Cancel this change"). Emails (`billing/emails.ts`) through the sign-in codes' Resend sender, to the owner and staff with the billing tick. Console: the last-days pop-up (`SizeDecisionPrompt`, in the shell), Plan card headline and next payment, the member bar, the waiting change with Cancel, how many to remove and by when with "Go to members", "your size stayed"; one Change size list; "places" is "members" everywhere.
- **Worst thing, first tests**: a stranger, a member, a trainer and a manager without the tick are refused (preview, change, cancel); the gym keeps 50 places after choosing 1; decided with 3 members it stays, nobody removed, the owner and billing manager emailed once; a join made while Paddle is making the change is refused; a join first keeps the size. **Six breaks, each RED, restored sha256-identical**: members not counted (2 red), the limit not held during the switch (1), the limit held from the choice (2), the billing check skipped (1), the fallback below the members (2), the fallback never used (1). A cut-off paid-trial change was red without its fix.
- **Verified**: api tsc 0 · eslint 0 · shared tsc 0 · lint 0 · 232/232 · billing route tests 47/47 · **whole api suite on `aihg_b`: 117 files, 3,701 passed, 1 failed** (orgs "currency with no price book", the known GBP clash; 153/153 alone) · the 3 web tests of the old card wording updated.
- **Run on Kd's sandbox through the real server, both builds**: gyms paid $140.45 for 500 and chose 200, Paddle untouched and the limit still 500; the worker at the decide time moved the gym with 0 members (next bill $86.01, $79 + tax, same date, no new transaction) and kept the gym with 201 members on 500 (Paddle untouched, next bill $140.45, 201 recorded). Paddle previews a prorated downgrade as a $54.44 credit. Test plans cancelled at Paddle; throwaway owners and members deleted from `aihg_b`. Web 2,680 passed (`poseAssets.contract` local Node 24 only).
- **Cost at 2,000 members** (2,592 MHz): the member count 1.8 ms, the limit read 0.2 ms, the worker's due scan 0.2 ms (its own index). The warning pass counts members once a run for each gym with a size due within 3 days until it is warned.
- **Kd's click-through of the pop-up passed** (*"all passed"*); he asked whether "Your size stayed…" ever goes: it now shows until the payment after the one it was decided for, or until the gym changes size (test red without it). His test gym put back (practice members removed, bill date restored).
- **Round one: 3 High, 2 Low, 4 weak tests, all fixed.** H1: a fitted size whose first Paddle attempt failed forgot the size asked for (no card line, no email) — the size asked for is kept on the row (`pending_requested_plan_id`, in `0045`) and every attempt decides from it. H2: a failed attempt kept holding the smaller limit and the card headed with it — the hold is let go on every failure but a lost answer, and the card heads with the plan's own size (`planSeatCap`). H3: billing emails reached a former billing manager — recipients now pass the routes' own check (`holdsPrivilege`). L1 "1 members". L2: a smaller size chosen while Paddle's renewal was unwritten was made at once with a credit — refused (`renewing`). Its test also found the 3-day warning sent after the decision time; now only before. Five breaks RED, restored identical.
- **The re-check closed all of round one and found N1 (High), made by H2's fix**: a paid trial's smaller size was not recounted after a failed attempt let go of its hold, so it could be made with more members than it holds. Every attempt now counts, trials included; a trial's has no fallback and is dropped (test red with the old line, restored identical). N2 (Low): the card named a remove-by time already past after a failed attempt; it names none then.
- **The second re-check closed N1 and N2 and the round** (*"Nothing Critical or High is open"*); its Low (a trial's "stays on" email read as if charged now) fixed after: a trial's says "from its first payment". Verified after: billing route tests 52/52; whole api suite 119 files, 3,741 passed, 1 failed (the known GBP clash).
- **Merged on Kd's word, 2026-09-25 (PR #112)**, CI green; ROADMAP ticked (this records PR). Next in Folder B: 1d; the two extra passes (security, integrity) run over Stage 3 item 1 when 1d is built, before a real gym pays. ROADMAP 1c-iii ticks on merge. Next in Folder B: 1d; the two extra passes run over Stage 3 item 1 when 1d is built.

## 2026-09-25 · 3a-vi-b: an app member follows their record (Folder A, branch `member-follows-record`, PR #111)

- **Risky (other people's records, a rule that picks), Opus xhigh; Kd said *"go"*.** A member who joined by invitation is matched to that invitation's record (`gym_members.entry_id`) before their email or phone: the upload's preview and confirm (`reconcile`'s `onListOf`), `membersAgainstList` (the list, Remove all, the person's page, Invite), the roster's "On your list as …" (current records only), the listed stamp. - **Verified**: api tsc 0 · eslint 0 · shared tsc 0 · lint 0 · 232/232 · member-list + invitation suites 36 files, 1,911 passed · full api suite 119 files, 3,697 passed, 2 failed: the Wes case (fixed, 21/21 alone) and orgs "currency with no price book" (the shared-seed clash, passed alone). Second month on the real server unchanged: 3 new · 2 missing; "Surname, First" 20 updated. Looked at in headless Edge on the real app.
- **Cost** (mains, 2,592 MHz; 10,000 × 2,000, every member's email changed): master names all 2,000 members as leaving, this job 0. Preview read again 184–568 ms over ten reads (master 344 ms; one 2,377 ms stall), Remove all's group 69–124 ms (master 224 ms), confirm's bystander worst 2.5–3.8 s (master 3.5 s), staged document 2.48 MB (master 2.09 MB: the record ids). At 200: preview read 40 ms, confirm 175 ms.

Three breaks RED, restored identical (always "taken off"; any record's name; the screen ignoring the reason). The roster page's bystander worst 65–89 ms against 40–66 ms without it at 10,000 × 2,000 (mains); member-list + invitation + orgs suites 37 files, 2,065 passed; web 2,638 passed (`poseAssets.contract` Node 24 only).

Now a joined member whose record is off is on through a current record on their email or phone WITH THAT RECORD'S NAME (3a-vi's same-name test), in all four places; Priya stays off (Arjun's name differs). L1: a grouping staged without record ids is worked out again. L2: "Put back on list" only for their own record taken off. L3: "taken off" only for the joined record. ## 2026-09-25 · 3a-vi: the same person next month (Folder A, branch `same-person-next-month`)

- **Risky (other people's records, a rule that picks), Opus xhigh; Kd said *"go and i agree recommendation"*** — the recommendation being 3a-vi-b, an app member matched by the invitation they joined with, as its own job before 5b (RULINGS 2026-09-25, ROADMAP).
- **Built**: `samePerson.ts` (`matchRows`, pure): all carried fields equal, else member number, else email (the name picks on a shared one), else phone; one row to one record; never one person on a different name AND different number/contact, or a different date of birth. `reconcile` uses it; the confirm finds a record by its old key and writes the new name, email, phone, member number and key; the four identity fields are hand-edit marks too. No migration, no new package. Spec §11.4 "Out of 3a-vi".
- **Outside cases** (a research pass, quotes in the test's header): PushPress and zingFit on families sharing a parent's email; Mindbody's editable client ID (SOK1234 → XSOK1234); GymMaster's reassigned key fobs; FCC's 35 million reassigned numbers a year; Zen Planner's export with no ID column. Two of them changed the rule before review (a member number alone neither joins nor parts two people).
- **Two refinements of the ruling, Kd: *"yes"* (RULINGS 2026-09-25)**; his click-through passed (*"all passed"*): a phone match also counts when both carry the same name (the ruling's own Emma, new email, is one person); a different date of birth parts two people on an email or phone match. Known edge by the ruling: with no date-of-birth column, a parent who left and a child new on that lone address read as one renamed record.
- **Worst thing, first tests**: a family at one parent's address stays two records; a daughter on a departed parent's address never takes the parent's record. **Three breaks, each RED, restored sha256-identical**: the name not picking on a shared address (4 unit + the route test — which first stayed GREEN because the dates of birth caught it; its second month now has no date column), the date-of-birth check removed (2 unit + route), a matching member number joining anyone (2 unit).
- **Verified**: api tsc 0 · eslint 0 · new suites 35/35 and 5/5 · by-hand routes 23/23 · full api suite 117 files, 3,665 passed, 1 failed (orgs "currency with no price book", passed alone, the shared-seed clash). Folder A's database was missing Folder B's `0043`: migrated and re-seeded.
- **Second month on the real server** (`.cost/scenarios-5a.ts`): 3 new · 2 missing · phone 2, email 1, name 1, status 4 (was 7 new, 6 missing); "Surname, First" 20 updated (was 20 new, 20 missing).
- **Cost** (mains, 2,592 MHz): at 10,000 × 2,000 members the confirm's bystander worst is 2,909–2,920 ms against master's 2,841 ms on the same month, preview 559–641 ms against 439 ms, within run-to-run noise; at 200 with every phone changed, confirm 134 ms, preview 57 ms.
- **Round one: 4 High, 2 Low, 3 weak tests, all fixed** (`8b02587`): the reviewer, on the real routes, got one person onto another's record four ways (a shared family email, "Keep them", a rename beating a later phone-and-name match, a fob or landline with a missing contact). Rebuilt, not patched: every carried field equal, then the same name with number/email/phone, then a rename only on a whole-list upload and a lone email; four breaks RED, restored identical. Member-list suites 26 files, 1,466 passed; second month still 3 new · 2 missing. The confirm at 10,000 now swings 2.8–6.8 s on master itself (disk stalls), this job 4.5–5.9 s: no measurable difference. Kd, asked whether a phone should accept a corrected spelling: kept split, the recommendation. The re-check closed the round.
- **Open**: ROADMAP 3a-vi ticks on merge. Next in Folder A: 3a-vi-b, then 5b.

## 2026-09-24 · Stage 3 item 1c-i (Folder B): Paddle's own page, and a failed payment (branch `paddle-manage-plan`)

- **Kd ruled first** (RULINGS 2026-09-24): a person pays only in the phone app, the web takes gyms' payments only, and paying in the app stays in the phone app's second version. 1b struck, 1d is Indian gyms only, 1c split into 1c-i (this) and 1c-ii (a bigger size, paying before the trial ends). Risky (money, Paddle's replies), Opus xhigh.
- **Built**: `POST /v1/orgs/:gymId/billing/portal` (billing staff only, this gym's own Paddle customer from our row, `no-store`, the link checked to be https on paddle.com); an overdue plan opens on the card, where Paddle takes what is owed (its docs, read that day). - **Risky (money, parses Paddle's replies), Opus xhigh.** Stage 3 item 1 split into 1a–1d. Kd ruled (RULINGS 2026-09-24): prices + tax; India through Razorpay (1d), not Paddle; Indian gyms ₹8,500 · 13,500 · 22,500 · 31,500 · 43,000 with no GST until he is registered.

A 500 on two presses at once was found by the log and fixed (a superseded checkout may have no Paddle id).
- **Outside cases**: Paddle's own SDK signature vectors (`billing.signature.test.ts`); Paddle's documented statuses for the rule's 110-case table (`billing.machine.test.ts`).
